# Acceptance Criteria - jupyterlab_workspace_manager_extension

The workspace panel that replaces the built-in Running panel: one accordion section per JupyterLab workspace with its tabs, terminals and kernels. Design in `docs/design/workspace-manager-design.html`, brief in `docs/brief.md`.

## Authors

- `@kj` Konrad Jelen

## Panel `PANEL`

The workspace panel in place of the built-in Running panel, its header and its reloads

- [x] `ACC-PANEL-1-REPLACE` **Replaces the built-in panel** - CRITICAL; plugin `@jupyterlab/running-extension:sidebar` is disabled; the workspace panel holds the sidebar place of Running Terminals and Kernels
  - evidence: 1 test(s) in Galata panel.spec.ts passed in full Galata rig run of final-verify (galata-full.log and galata-full.json, 91 of 91 passed, 6 workers, --retries=0)
  - test: Galata panel.spec.ts: load lab, assert one sidebar tab `jp-running-sessions` showing the workspace panel and no `.jp-RunningSessions` section list
  - test-tags: FUNCTIONAL
  - log: 2026-10-23T13:58:15Z @kj added
  - log: 2026-10-24T10:03:58Z @kj closed; reason: final verification of the implementation, 2026-10-24
- [x] `ACC-PANEL-2-RESTORE` **Saved layouts restore the panel** - HIGH; a saved layout whose left sidebar showed the Running panel opens the workspace panel in the same place; restorer name stays `running-sessions`
  - evidence: 1 test(s) in Galata panel.spec.ts passed in full Galata rig run of final-verify (galata-full.log and galata-full.json, 91 of 91 passed, 6 workers, --retries=0)
  - test: Galata panel.spec.ts: save layout with left current `running-sessions`, reload, assert workspace panel visible
  - test-tags: FUNCTIONAL
  - log: 2026-10-23T13:58:15Z @kj added
  - log: 2026-10-24T10:03:58Z @kj closed; reason: final verification of the implementation, 2026-10-24
- [x] `ACC-PANEL-3-SHOWCMD` **Show panel command** - MEDIUM; command `running:show-panel` exists and shows the workspace panel
  - evidence: 1 test(s) in Galata panel.spec.ts passed in full Galata rig run of final-verify (galata-full.log and galata-full.json, 91 of 91 passed, 6 workers, --retries=0)
  - test: Galata panel.spec.ts: hide the sidebar, execute `running:show-panel`, assert panel visible
  - test-tags: FUNCTIONAL
  - log: 2026-10-23T13:58:15Z @kj added
  - log: 2026-10-24T10:03:58Z @kj closed; reason: final verification of the implementation, 2026-10-24
- [x] `ACC-PANEL-4-OTHERS` **Sections of other extensions** - HIGH; a section another extension registers through IRunningSessionManagers is not listed in the panel, which shows the workspace cards and the Kernels, Terminals and Language servers sections only

  - test: Galata panel.spec.ts: assert the panel's headers are the workspace cards, KERNELS, TERMINALS and LANGUAGE SERVERS, and no header of another manager
  - test-tags: UNIT, FUNCTIONAL

  - evidence: 1 test(s) in Jest panel.spec.ts passed in make test of final-verify (make-test.log, Jest 41 of 41 passed)
  - test: Jest panel.spec.ts: fake timers, hold the requests, assert disabled and turning class, release, assert enabled after 500 ms
  - test-tags: UNIT
  - log: 2026-10-23T13:58:15Z @kj added
  - log: 2026-10-24T10:03:58Z @kj closed; reason: final verification of the implementation, 2026-10-24
- [x] `ACC-PANEL-8-POLL` **Reload while visible** - MEDIUM; while the panel is visible it reloads every 10 s, without the turning icon; a reload, by the poll or the refresh button, keeps each section's scroll position and open state, the keyboard focus, the selected row, the open card and an open menu
  - evidence: Jest panel.spec.ts ACC-PANEL-8-POLL (selected row kept) passed in make-test-rd3.log (Jest 80 of 80, pytest 103); Galata panel.spec.ts ACC-PANEL-8-POLL and render.spec.ts HDR-08 (selected TERMINALS row kept over two polls) passed in galata-rd3.log (159 of 159, --retries=0); the Jest test and HDR-08 failed on the pre-change code (make-test-rd3-red.log, galata-rd3-red.log)
  - test: Jest panel.spec.ts: fake timers, advance 10 s, assert one reload and no turning class; with a section list scrolled, KERNELS closed, a row selected and focused, a card open and the ... menu open, advance 10 s, assert all kept; Galata panel.spec.ts: PUT 30 workspaces, scroll the WORKSPACES list, wait 11 s, assert its scrollTop unchanged; focus Refresh, press Enter, assert the focus stays on it
  - test-tags: UNIT, FUNCTIONAL

  - test-tags: UNIT

  - log: 2026-10-24T10:03:59Z @kj closed; reason: final verification of the implementation, 2026-10-24

  - mechanism: 2026-10-24T03:00:56Z @kj the CI job integration-tests installs galaxalabs_jupyterlab_steel_dark_theme from PyPI, so the test finds Galaxa Dark Theme - Steel there too

- [x] `ACC-PANEL-74-TEXT-SIZE` **Text size** - MEDIUM; no text is smaller than `--jp-ui-font-size0`; size 0 is used only by the panel title, the section labels and counts, the group labels, the empty states, and a row's time, status word and detail values; every other text is at least `--jp-ui-font-size1`
  - evidence: Galata legibility.spec.ts ACC-PANEL-74-TEXT-SIZE passed in tmp/workflows/sections/logs/galata-rd1.log (158 of 158 passed, --retries=0); its size 0 assertion failed on the pre-change frontend (tmp/workflows/sections/logs/galata-rd1-red-size.log)
  - test: Galata legibility.spec.ts: walk all text nodes; assert font size >= resolved `--jp-ui-font-size0` everywhere and >= `--jp-ui-font-size1` outside the title, section headers, group labels, empty states and row meta (time, status word, detail values), which are asserted at size0 in both themes
  - test-tags: FUNCTIONAL

  - test-tags: UNIT, FUNCTIONAL
  - mechanism: 2026-10-28T17:13:41Z @kj the terminals route lists only a terminal whose pty is alive (`pty.ptyproc.terminated` false and `isalive()` true) and adds no fingerprint; the panel renders exactly the terminals the route lists, so a shut-down terminal leaves its rows and card counts at the next load in every window
  - mechanism: 2026-10-27T20:30:06Z @kj the terminals route reads each pty fingerprint '<pid>:<create time>' once and keeps it while the pty lives, since terminado reaps the process before DELETE answers 204 (bare pid without a start time, null without a pty); the panel maps each terminal it shut down from name to fingerprint, hides a terminal while both match, drops an entry once the name is gone or the fingerprint differs
  - mechanism: 2026-10-27T20:11:35Z @kj the terminals route adds the pty fingerprint '<pid>:<create time>' (bare pid without a start time, null without a pty); the panel maps each terminal it shut down from name to fingerprint; _model hides a terminal while both match; a load drops an entry whose name is gone or fingerprint differs
  - mechanism: 2026-10-27T18:39:07Z @kj the panel keeps the names of terminals it shut down; _model leaves them out of every render; a load that no longer lists a name drops it
